| EC153 | Low | Clear formatting over a coloured highlight left its emoji behind (twelfth re-audit) | Fixed | Only the `==` marks were taken | The colour prefix goes with the opening mark | `highlight-colour.test.ts` "takes the colour off along with the marks" (fails before) |
| EC154 | Low | Sort or Reverse on a numbered list of ten or more items could push a child out of its item (twelfth re-audit, from EC129) | Fixed | Renumbering changed the marker's width without moving the item's later lines | The item's indented lines move by the change in width | `lines.test.ts` "a child moves in when its item's number grows a digit" (fails before) |
| EC155 | Medium | Marks across paragraphs left out any line that starts with inline code, so code toggled on over two paragraphs could not be taken off (thirteenth re-audit) | Fixed | The per-block split skipped every line whose start sits in any code, inline code included | It skips only lines in a fenced or indented code block or a table | `marks-code-lines.test.ts` (2 tests, fail before) |
| EC156 | Medium | A coloured highlight pressed twice at a caret gave `==🔴 🔴 ==`, and another colour added a second emoji (thirteenth re-audit, gap in EC149) | Fixed | The colour of an empty highlight read as plain | An inner text that is only a colour emoji is read as that colour, so the same colour takes it off and another swaps it | `highlight-caret.test.ts` (2 tests, fail before) |
| EC157 | Medium | Heading N and heading up or down over a selection holding a code fence or table wrote `## ` on its lines (thirteenth re-audit, EC92 in a sibling command) | Fixed | The heading commands changed every non-blank selected line | Lines in a code block or table are left alone | `heading-verbatim.test.ts` (2 tests, fail before) |
| EC158 | Medium | Pasting several lines into a fence inside a list item or quote put the later lines at column 0, which ended the item and left an unclosed fence swallowing the rest of the note (thirteenth re-audit) | Fixed | A paste into code went to the plain paste, without the container lead | The fence's container lead goes after each line break, for the keys and the menu; the code itself is kept as is | `paste-menu.test.ts` "both keep the later lines inside the list item", "both keep the later lines inside the quote" (fail before) |
| EC159 | Medium | Sort or Reverse lines with the caret in a code block sorted the fence lines in too, breaking the block; in a table it sorted the header (thirteenth re-audit) | Fixed | The run was every non-blank line around the caret | In code the run is the code lines between the fences, in a table its body rows; outside, the run stops at code and table lines | `lines.test.ts` "sorting and reversing lines in code or a table" (7 tests, fail before) |
| EC160 | Medium | Typing a mark over several already marked blocks took their marks off, though over one paragraph the same key adds one (thirteenth re-audit, from EC147) | Fixed | Typing used the toggling command across blocks | Typed marks across blocks always add | `typed-marks-blocks.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC161 | Medium | Pressing Home again at the start of an item's words, or clicking its bullet, sent the caret to the end of the line above (thirteenth re-audit, regression from EC150) | Fixed | The move to the line above, meant for ArrowLeft, fired for every move that started at the words | Moves that land in the hidden marker go to the words; ArrowLeft from the words is its own key binding that goes to the line above | `item-start-again.test.ts` (4 tests) |
| EC162 | Medium | A table of contents entry put the caret before a heading's hidden `## ` with quiet marks on (thirteenth re-audit, gap in EC150) | Fixed | The jump was not marked as a selection, so the marker correction never saw it | The jump is a selection like any other | `toc-jump.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC163 | Medium | In Read-only mode Find selected and counted a match inside a drawn table, maths or diagram, but never showed it (thirteenth re-audit) | Fixed | Read-only mode reveals nothing, so the block around the match stayed drawn | The match Find selected is revealed in Read-only mode too, until the selection moves | `find-read-only.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC164 | Low | Bold or other marks over a whole footnote definition wrapped its `[^1]: ` label (thirteenth re-audit) | Fixed | The line opening skipped headings, items and quotes, not footnote labels | Footnote labels are skipped too | `marks-footnote.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC165 | Medium | Numbered headings showed wrong numbers in any note longer than a screen or two: they restarted once the top was scrolled out (thirteenth re-audit) | Fixed | The numbers were style counters, which only count the lines the editor has drawn | Each heading's number is worked out from the whole note and written on its line, and the style shows that | `modes.test.ts` "numbered headings" (4 tests, fail before) |
| EC166 | Low | An edit in an HTML block's opener left the blocks below it stale (thirteenth re-audit) | Fixed | A letter typed or taken out there was treated as prose, and an HTML block that is not a card has no span | An edit on a line with a `<` takes the local rebuild | `blocks.test.ts` "a letter taken out of an HTML block opener draws the maths the block no longer holds" (fails before) |
| EC167 | Medium | Words typed on the line opened under a table that ends the note (a click below the note, ArrowDown or Escape out of the last row, Insert table at the end) became another row of the table (fourteenth re-audit) | Fixed | That line was opened with a single line break, and a non-blank line right under a table is one of its rows | The line under a closing table is opened a blank line away | `tail.test.ts` "words written under a table that ends the note are no row of it"; `navigation.test.ts` "made under a table that ends the document takes words that are no row of it" (fail before) |
| EC168 | Medium | Bold, italic, highlight, typed marks, Mod-K and a pasted link over a callout's title line wrapped `[!NOTE]` and broke the callout (fourteenth re-audit, EC125 for callouts) | Fixed | The line opening skipped heading, item, task and footnote openings, not a callout marker | A quoted callout marker is part of the opening | `marks-callout.test.ts` (4 tests, fail before) |
| EC169 | Medium | Heading commands over front matter, a `$$` block or an HTML block wrote `## ` on its lines, which wiped the note's properties (fourteenth re-audit, gap in EC157) | Fixed | Only fenced and indented code and tables counted as blocks to leave alone | Front matter, maths, HTML and comment blocks count too | `heading-blocks.test.ts` "front matter keeps its lines" and two more (fail before) |
| EC170 | Medium | Sort lines in front matter, a `$$` block or a comment sorted its delimiter lines in, losing the properties or leaving a comment open over the rest of the note (fourteenth re-audit, gap in EC159) | Fixed | Same gap as EC169 | Those blocks sort only their inner lines | `sort-verbatim.test.ts` (3 tests, fail before) |
| EC171 | Medium | Heading commands on a heading or paragraph inside a list item doubled the hashes, could not take a heading off, or moved the paragraph out of the item (fourteenth re-audit) | Fixed | The heading was read only at column 0, past quote marks | The words of a line start after an item marker that holds a heading, and after the indent of an item's later lines | `heading-blocks.test.ts` "a heading written in an item changes level instead of gaining a second run of hashes" and two more (fail before) |
| EC172 | Medium | Typing `- ` or `1. ` in front of a `$$` line hid the rest of the note behind a broken maths picture (fourteenth re-audit) | Fixed | The space was treated as prose, and the maths it closed again sat lines away from the edit | An edit on a line with `$$` takes the local rebuild | `maths-item-marker.test.ts` (2 tests, fail before) |
| EC173 | Medium | A note embed kept showing the embedded note's old words after it was saved, and after it was locked (fourteenth re-audit) | Fixed | The embed compared only its path and link, so it was never drawn again | It also compares the note index, as query blocks do | `embed-refresh.test.ts` (2 tests, fail before) |
| EC174 | Medium | At the start of a right-to-left list item, ArrowLeft jumped to the line above and ArrowRight was stuck (fourteenth re-audit, from EC161) | Fixed | The back-out key was bound to ArrowLeft whatever the line's direction | It is bound to the key that goes backward on that line | `item-start-rtl.test.ts` (2 tests, fail before) |
| EC175 | Medium | Outline and palette jumps put the caret before a heading's hidden `## ` with quiet marks on (fourteenth re-audit, found in the app, as EC162) | Fixed | The app's jump was not marked as a selection | The jump moved into the editor as `landOnLine`, a selection like any other | `landing.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC176 | Low | In Read-only mode Find never showed a match inside a picture's description (fourteenth re-audit, gap in EC163) | Fixed | The picture's own reveal check ignored the found match | It uses the same reveal rule | `find-read-only-image.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC177 | Low | Copy link on a code fence inside a list item wrote `^id` at column 0, which split the list (fourteenth re-audit) | Fixed | The id line had no indent | It takes the item's content indent | `bulk.test.ts` "a fence in a list item takes it inside the item, so the list stays one list" (fails before) |
| EC178 | Medium | An AI ask or a Run fence written on the list item line itself (`- ```ai`) streamed nothing and lost its output on the next edit (fifteenth re-audit) | Fixed | The AI and Run code each had their own fence pattern that only knew fences at the start of a line | Both use one `opensFence` that steps over quote and item markers | `ai-item-line.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC179 | Medium | Enter on an empty line inside code in a list item dropped the item indent, so the code fell out of the item (fifteenth re-audit) | Fixed | The new line copied the empty line's own (missing) indent | An empty line takes the indent the fence opener holds | `enter-blank-code.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC180 | Medium | Bold over a word picked inside a bold run wrapped it in more stars instead of taking the bold off it (fifteenth re-audit) | Fixed | The toggle only looked at marks right around the selection | A selection inside a run splits that word out of the run | `bold-inside.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC181 | Medium | Heading commands on a callout title line put `## ` before `[!note]`, which broke the callout (fifteenth re-audit) | Fixed | The line's words were taken to start right after the quote marks | The callout marker is stepped over too | `heading-callout.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC182 | Medium | Highlight with the caret inside a highlighted word put empty `====` inside it instead of taking it off (fifteenth re-audit) | Fixed | A caret was only checked against marks right beside it | The highlight run around the caret is found from the syntax tree | `highlight-inside.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC183 | Medium | A note embed blanked and redrew itself on every save of the note around it (fifteenth re-audit, regression from EC173) | Fixed | The widget compared the whole note index, so each save made a new widget | The widget is kept and refreshes its words in place | `embed-steady.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC184 | Medium | Words after an HTML comment or a web frame that opens a line were hidden in Live Preview, though the reading view shows them (fifteenth re-audit) | Fixed | The whole HTML block was covered by the comment or frame widget | Only the comment up to `-->`, or the frame tag, is covered | `comment-words.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC185 | Medium | A note embed right under a heading or front matter was not drawn (fifteenth re-audit) | Fixed | The embed asked for a blank line on both sides | A heading or front matter next to it counts as its own block | `embed-under-heading.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC186 | Medium | In a shared note a block whose source you were editing snapped back to its drawn form whenever a partner moved or typed (fifteenth re-audit) | Fixed | Every change from outside reset the reveal choice, meant only for a note being swapped in | The choice resets only when the whole note is replaced | `peer-redraw.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC187 | Medium | Clear formatting over a selection that starts or ends inside a bold, italic, struck, highlighted or code run deleted one of its marks and left the other, so the rest of the line changed style (fifteenth re-audit) | Fixed | Each mark was cut to the selection and only the mark characters inside it were deleted | Runs are read as open and close pairs; a run that goes on past the selection is closed or reopened at its edge | `marks-callout.test.ts` "clear formatting over a selection that starts or ends inside a run" (fails before) |
| EC188 | Medium | Join lines deleted text that only looked like a marker, such as `2024. was` in a paragraph or `- x` in code (fifteenth re-audit) | Fixed | The next line's lead was stripped by a pattern, without the syntax tree | Only quote, list, task and heading marks the tree places there are stripped | `lines.test.ts` "joining lines" (fails before) |
| EC189 | Low | Copy link on a fence written on the item line itself (`- ```js`) wrote `^id` at column 0, which split the list (fifteenth re-audit, gap in EC177) | Fixed | The item was skipped when it started on the fence's own line | An item that starts on the block's first line counts | `bulk.test.ts` "a fence written on the item line itself takes it inside the item too" (fails before) |
| EC190 | Low | Pasting two sentences with a year between commas turned them into a table (fifteenth re-audit) | Fixed | One number between commas was enough to count as data | Comma and space text is prose when a line ends a sentence or no cell is a number | `paste.test.ts` "two sentences with a year between commas in each stay prose" (fails before) |
| EC191 | Medium | Bold or italic over a word inside a `_` or `__` run split it with stars, so `_one two three_` became `_one* two *three_` (sixteenth re-audit, gap in EC180) | Fixed | The split wrote the command's own mark, not the run's | The run's written mark closes and reopens it | `underscore-runs.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC192 | Medium | Highlight over a word picked inside a highlight nested the marks: `==one ==two== three==` (sixteenth re-audit, gap in EC182) | Fixed | The run around the selection was only looked for with a caret | A picked word inside a run is split out of it, or given the new colour | `highlight-picked.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC193 | Medium | In table cells, bold, code or highlight with the caret or a word inside a marked run wrote empty marks (`**wo****rd**`) (sixteenth re-audit, EC2, EC180 and EC182 again) | Fixed | Cell commands ran on a state with no Markdown language, so the syntax tree was empty | Cell commands run on a parsed Markdown state | `table/cell-marks.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC194 | Medium | Enter after a new fence typed above a later code block wrote no closer, so the rest of the note down to that block turned into code (sixteenth re-audit) | Fixed | The later block's closer was taken as this fence's own | A fence whose body holds a later opener with a language counts as open | `fence-above-block.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC195 | Medium | Footnote definitions and definition details were drawn as raw source: their bold, links and wikilinks showed as typed and could not be followed (sixteenth re-audit) | Fixed | Their block parsers built the node with only its marks and never parsed the rest of the line inline | The rest of the line is parsed inline into the node | `footnote-words.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC196 | Low | A note embed right under a `---` rule or a code block's closing fence was not drawn (sixteenth re-audit, gap in EC185) | Fixed | Only headings and front matter counted as a block of their own | Rules and code blocks count too | `embed-apart.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC197 | Medium | Enter after a new fence typed above a later code block with no language wrote no closer, so the old block's code turned into prose and its closer opened code to the end of the note (seventeenth re-audit, gap in EC194) | Fixed | Only a later opener with a language was taken as a sign the new fence stole a closer | A later fence left open, or not part of any code block, counts too | `fence-later-bare.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC198 | Medium | Taking bold, italic or a highlight off a word followed by punctuation inside a run (`**apples, pears, plums**`) left stray marks and unbolded the rest (seventeenth re-audit, gap in EC180, EC191 and EC192) | Fixed | The new marks only stepped over spaces, so they landed next to the punctuation where they cannot open or close | The marks step over spaces and punctuation to hug the words | `split-punctuation.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC199 | Medium | Paste as plain text into code inside a list item or quote put later lines at column 0, which ended the item and left a fence open (seventeenth re-audit, EC158 for the plain paste) | Fixed | Plain paste inserted the text as it was | It keeps the code's lead on every line, as a normal paste does | `paste-plain-code.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC200 | Medium | Turn into deleted a leading number and dot: `## 1. Introduction` as Heading 1 became `# Introduction`, and a paragraph line starting `2024. ` lost it (seventeenth re-audit) | Fixed | A list marker was stripped from every line, after a heading's hashes and on lines that are no list item | A marker is stripped only where the syntax tree has a list item, never after hashes | `block/turn-into-numbers.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC201 | High | Drawn table cells dropped every bare web address, `www.` address, `<https://…>` autolink and email, so `| see https://x.test now |` showed "see  now" (seventeenth re-audit) | Fixed | The cell renderer hid every URL node, not only a link's address | Addresses are shown as links; only a link's own address is hidden | `table/cell-address.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC202 | Medium | A web address inside a link's words was hidden and taken as the link's target: `[https://example.com](https://example.com)` showed nothing, `[Visit www.x.com now](…)` opened `www.x.com` as a note, and a picture whose description held an address loaded that address (seventeenth re-audit) | Fixed | Every URL in a link was concealed, and the first URL child was read as the address | One `linkAddress` finds the URL after `](`; only that one is hidden or followed | `link-words.test.ts` "a link whose words are a web address", "a picture whose description holds a web address" (fails before) |

| EC204 | Medium | Taking a mark off a word next to a link, wikilink or maths inside a run put the mark inside that markup: `**see [[Note]] now**` gave `**see [[Note**]] now` (eighteenth re-audit, regression from EC198) | Fixed | The split stepped over every punctuation character, `]]`, `)` and `$` included | The split steps over punctuation only where it is plain words of the run, and only as far as a mark can close or open | `split-links.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC205 | Medium | Taking a mark off a word that only punctuation separates from the run's edge wrote empty marks: `**Note (important)**` gave `**Note** (important)****` (eighteenth re-audit, regression from EC198) | Fixed | Reaching the run's edge still inserted a mark beside the run's own | Reaching the edge takes the run's own mark off instead | `split-links.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC206 | Medium | Mod-B, typing, Mod-B again took the bold off the words just typed instead of stepping out (eighteenth re-audit) | Fixed | A caret right before a run's closing marks counted as inside the run | There the caret steps out past the closing marks | `bold-typed.test.ts` (fails before) |

| EC213 | Medium | A caret or selection right at the outer edge of inline code counted as in code: rich paste was skipped, `*` over words ending in a code span replaced them, and an address pasted over them was not linked (eighteenth re-audit) | Fixed | The code check resolved into a code span that only touched the edge | Only a position between the span's backticks, or in a code block, counts | `paste-code.test.ts`, `wrap.test.ts`, `paste.test.ts` (fail before) |
| EC214 | Medium | Highlight, type, highlight again took the highlight (and its colour) off the words just typed instead of stepping out (nineteenth re-audit, EC206 for highlight) | Fixed | A caret right before a highlight's closing marks counted as inside it | There the caret steps out past the closing marks | `highlight-step-out.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC215 | Medium | Sort lines and Reverse lines over a selected list kept old numbers and moved children away from their items (nineteenth re-audit, EC129 for a selection) | Fixed | Items were used only for a caret; a selection sorted every line alone | A selection inside one list sorts the items it touches and renumbers them | `sort-picked-list.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC216 | Medium | A `www.` address with `@` or `:` in its path opened as a mail (`mailto:www.youtube.com/@mkbhd`) or was not a link in a cell (nineteenth re-audit, regression from EC201 and EC207) | Fixed | The scheme was picked from `:` and `@` anywhere in the address, and the email pattern let `/` in | One `bareHref` checks `www.` and a scheme first, and an email has no `/` | `addresses.test.ts` (fails before) |

| EC247 | Medium | Enter at the end of a folded numbered item, with items below, still gave its hidden children to the new item (twenty-fifth re-audit, gap in EC243) | Fixed | The fold fix applied only when the edit was a single insert, and renumbering adds more | The new item moves past the fold and the renumbering below is kept | `enter-folded-numbered.test.ts` (fails before) |

| EC249 | Medium | With marks out of sight, Backspace at the start of words opening with a mark glued the list, heading or task marker to them, and Backspace or Delete at a line edge took hidden stars instead of joining lines (twenty-fifth re-audit, regression from EC242) | Fixed | The step over hidden marks went on past a hidden block marker and left line edges to the default delete | At a line edge or a hidden block marker the caret steps over the marks and the usual Backspace or Delete runs from there; a last letter next to the caret's own closing marks takes the whole run | `quiet-marks-edges.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC250 | Medium | Ctrl+Backspace or Ctrl+Delete (Alt on Mac) beside a marked word, a link or a wikilink took the hidden marks and broke it (twenty-fifth re-audit) | Fixed | The library groups raw characters, so hidden marks were their own group | Word delete that reaches hidden marks takes the whole marked run or link | `word-delete-marks.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC251 | Medium | An embed of a block id on the second line of a wrapped list item still showed the whole list (twenty-fifth re-audit, gap in EC245) | Fixed | Only an id on the item's own line was read as an item | The item holding the id line is found above it | `wikilink/embed-wrapped-item.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC252 | Medium | Enter at the end of a folded item whose line ends in a space still gave its hidden children to the new item (twenty-sixth re-audit, gap in EC247) | Fixed | The list continuation first trims trailing spaces, so its change began before the fold | A change that trims only spaces up to the fold still has its new item moved past the fold | `enter-folded-trailing.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC253 | Medium | Upper, lower or title case over a wikilink to a block recased its `#^id`, so the link and embed stopped resolving (twenty-sixth re-audit) | Fixed | Only a target inside the opening mark (aliased links) was kept | The `#^id` part of every wikilink is kept as written | `case-block-link.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC254 | Medium | With marks out of sight, Backspace at the visible start of a numbered item's marked words glued the number to them (twenty-sixth re-audit, gap in EC249) | Fixed | The stop for hidden block markers only knew bullets and tasks, whose markers hide | The start of any item's words is a stop too | `quiet-numbered-backspace.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC255 | Medium | Word delete beside a footnote mark, underlined words or an autolink took the hidden markup (twenty-sixth re-audit, gap in EC250) | Fixed | Those were not known as runs | Footnote marks and autolinks are runs, and a hidden HTML tag is taken with its partner | `word-delete-hidden.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC256 | Medium | After indenting the note's first line into code and back (or typing four spaces there and undoing), the line stayed drawn as code (twenty-sixth re-audit) | Fixed | `@lezer/markdown` reused a fragment cut at the note's start, keeping the old code block | The parser patch no longer reuses a fragment whose start was cut when parsing from position 0 | `first-line-reparse.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC257 | Medium | Heading commands with only a caret wrote hashes into a table row, code, an HTML block or front matter (twenty-seventh re-audit, gap in EC157 and EC169) | Fixed | One-line selections returned before the verbatim-block filter | Lines in verbatim blocks are left out for every selection | `heading-caret-verbatim.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC258 | Medium | Word delete next to a bold, italic, underlined or highlighted run of several words deleted the whole run (twenty-seventh re-audit, from EC250) | Fixed | The run was taken as one unit | In runs of words only the nearest word goes, with the space before it, and the marks stay; links, code, maths and footnote marks still go as a unit | `word-delete-run.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC259 | Medium | Text with Windows line ends pasted with Paste as plain text, Edit > Paste or onto an empty item was dropped near the note's end or left the caret one place too far per line (twenty-eighth re-audit) | Fixed | The caret was measured on the raw text, while CodeMirror writes `\r\n` as one line feed | Pasted text has its line ends turned into line feeds before it is placed | `paste-windows-lines.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC260 | Medium | Word delete next to a marked run whose edge word held other markup (a nested mark, a link, code, a colour, a line break) left broken markup (twenty-eighth re-audit, regression from EC258) | Fixed | The nearest word was cut from the raw inner text | The nearest unit is a plain word or a whole nested node, after any highlight colour, and a line break before it is kept | `word-delete-nested.test.ts` (fails before) |

| EC262 | Medium | In a right-to-left note, ticked tasks, callout titles, aliased wikilinks and emoji-led headings were drawn left to right (twenty-ninth re-audit) | Fixed | The line direction was read from the raw text, so the box letter, callout type, link target or shortcode counted as the first strong letter | The direction is read from the words shown, past task boxes, callout types, link targets, addresses, HTML tags and shortcodes | `direction-past-marks.test.ts` (fails before) |

| EC297 | Medium | A block embed or hover card of a table, code or paragraph named by an id on its own line showed the heading above it too, and an id right under a closing fence or on an older build's closing fence gave an empty or bare-fence embed (fortieth re-audit, gap and regression in EC296) | Fixed | The section above an own-line id widened over every non-blank line, and the paragraph widening refused to join a fence | One block-bounds helper is shared: a fence takes its whole fence, a table its rows, a heading or rule stands alone, and a paragraph stops at those and at list and quote starts | `wikilink/embed-block-sections.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC298 | Medium | Picking a list item in the `[[Note#^` block completion named the list's last item only, and picking a heading block wrote an id onto the heading that broke `[[Note#Title]]` links (fortieth re-audit) | Fixed | `blocksOf` made one block per run of lines and `namedBlock` put the id at the end of that run; a heading's name kept its block id | Each list item is a block of its own and is named on its own words; a heading's name leaves out a trailing block id, so `[[Note#Title]]` still finds a named heading | `wikilink/block-complete-items.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC299 | Medium | The note hover card's editable preview opened at the first place the section's first line appears, not at the linked block or heading (fortieth re-audit) | Fixed | `wikilink/hover.ts` `anchorOf` used `source.indexOf` of the section's first line | The preview opens at the whole line that starts the longest run of the section's lines | `wikilink/hover-anchor.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC300 | Medium | Naming a block from a line of words right above a list, or from the second line of a wrapped item, named the last item of the list instead (forty-first re-audit) | Fixed | `@orden/markdown` `block-names.ts` `endOf` only stopped at the next item when the named line was itself an item, and continuation lines were not traced to their item | The walk stops at any item line, and a line inside an item names that item | `block-naming-bounds.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC301 | Medium | Naming a list item that holds a code fence wrote `^id` on its own line, which broke the list, and one case wrote the id into the code (forty-first re-audit) | Fixed | `block-names.ts` treated the item's fence as a separate block and the fence's dash lines as items | An item is named as a whole, with the id on the item's own line (or after the run when it must stand apart), never inside its code | `block-naming-bounds.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC302 | Medium | A heading named by a block link (`# Groceries ^id`) was no longer found by heading links when the note opened and showed the id in the outline (forty-first re-audit) | Fixed | The app's `outline.ts` `scanHeadings` kept the raw heading text, unlike every other heading reader | The outline reads heading text through the shared `headingText`, which drops a trailing id | `apps/desktop` `outline.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC303 | Medium | An id on its own line under a list (`^groceries` after a blank line) embedded and hovered only the last item, a regression from EC297 (forty-first re-audit) | Fixed | `links.ts` `blockAbove` used `blockAround`, whose prose spread stops at every item | An id under a list names the whole list, from its first item down | `wikilink/list-own-line-id.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC304 | Medium | Picking a list item in the block completion linked to the line of words above the list, or to the wrapped item before it, and could take that item's name (forty-second re-audit, regression from EC300) | Fixed | `@orden/markdown` `links.ts` `blockAround` spread upward from an item into the lines above it, so `blocksOf` started the item's block on the previous block | An item's block starts at the item and takes only its own continuation lines | `wikilink/block-complete-list-items.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC305 | Medium | Naming a heading with words right under it named the words, and naming words right above a heading or a quote named the heading or quote (forty-second re-audit) | Fixed | `block-names.ts` `endOf` walked on across headings and quote edges | A heading is named alone, and the walk stops at a heading or where a quote starts or ends | `block-naming-neighbours.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC306 | Medium | An id on the line right under a list item embedded the whole list, and an id under a list with blank lines between items showed only its tail (forty-second re-audit, gaps in EC303) | Fixed | `links.ts` `blockAbove` widened to the list even with no blank line, and `listStart` stopped at blank lines | An id touching an item names that item; an id after a blank line names the whole list, loose or tight | `own-line-id-reach.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC307 | Medium | The hover card opened at an earlier block with the same words (a repeated task or paragraph), not at the linked block (forty-second re-audit, case EC299 left open) | Fixed | `wikilink/hover.ts` found the section again by its text, and a tie went to the first match | A block link opens at the line its section starts on (`sectionLine`), with the text match kept for headings | `wikilink/hover-anchor-repeated.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC308 | Medium | Naming words right above a code fence or a rule, or a setext heading, linked to the code, the rule, the underline or the words below instead (forty-third re-audit) | Fixed | `block-names.ts` `endOf` walked on into fences, rules and setext underlines, and `links.ts` `blockAround` read a dash underline as a block of its own | Naming stops before a fence or rule, a setext heading is named with its underline and nothing after it, and a block read from its underline takes the heading | `block-naming-touching.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC309 | Medium | A numbered or bulleted list pasted from Word, whose plain text puts a tab after each marker, became a table (forty-third re-audit) | Fixed | `paste.ts` tried tab-separated text as a table before reading the HTML | Tab-separated text is not made a table when the HTML is a list and holds no table | `paste-word-list-tabs.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC310 | Medium | Turn into Task un-ticked done tasks (forty-third re-audit) | Fixed | `block/shape.ts` stripped `[x]` with the marker and wrote a fresh `- [ ] ` | A ticked task keeps its tick | `block/turn-into-ticked.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC311 | Medium | In a note with Windows line endings the hover card opened one character further per line above the target, or blank near the end of the note (forty-third re-audit) | Fixed | `wikilink/hover.ts` counted offsets over the raw text with `\r` kept, while the card's editor counts a line break as one | Landing offsets count each line break once | `wikilink/hover-anchor-crlf.test.ts` (fails before) |
| EC312 | Medium | A table row ending in a block id (`\| 1 \| 2 \| ^t1`) drew an extra column, and the first cell edit moved the id inside the pipes, breaking links to it (forty-third re-audit) | Fixed | `table/model.ts` `parseTable` read the id as a cell | The table model keeps each row's id beside it through edits, row moves, sorts and column changes, and writes it back after the row | `table/row-block-id.test.ts` (fails before) |

### Editor core: open Lows

Seen, judged minor, left as they are:

- A `data:` picture pasted from a web page is written inline into the note.
- A dropped file of a kind Orden cannot hold does nothing, with no message.
- Ticking a checkbox during an IME composition.
- An autosave and a rename landing at the same moment.
- Smart punctuation and `leaveQuote`/`closeFence` act on the main cursor only with several cursors.
- `leaveQuote` on an empty quote line inside a list item drops the item's indent.
- Changing a list's kind does not renumber following siblings outside the selection.
- The task hint and slash commands are offered in front matter.
- An AI answer streaming into a note that is also open in a second tab follows only the tab it started in.
- Writes that started before a note turned read-only (a table cell, a language) still land.
- A snippet trigger typed in full and then Enter accepts the snippet instead of a new line, as completions do.
- Ctrl-Space completion is taken by the input method switcher on some Windows and Linux setups.
- An unclosed `$$` makes the rest of the note maths until it is closed; a callout marker written mid-line is plain text.
- `later` drops a write when the note changed while a field was open and the pane then moved (it could rebase instead).
- The link preview card can send two writes from the same base when the card is let go during the disk read of the first.
- Enter in code inside a quote or list reads a `>` or list marker in the code's own text as a container and adds spaces inside the code.
- Pending cell words are dropped when another writer edits the first cell of a table written without a leading pipe.
- An alt typed as `size\|300` is written so that it reads back as a size.
- A task box pressed in a preview tab in the moment before the tab takes on another note, while the tick code is first loading, can tick the same line in the new note.
- A `$` before a letter with no closing `$` yet (`set $HOME to "x"`) keeps straight quotes for the rest of the line, since maths may still be closed.
- Taking off an item in the middle or at the end of a list joins its words to the item above as a lazy line, as plain Markdown reads it.
- A tab or five spaces after a list marker are counted as their characters, not their columns, when a list changes kind.
- Lines of one quote written with different spaces before `>` are not seen as the same quote when a list is made inside it.
- An `<img>` with a stray quote in an unquoted value (`alt=it's`) is not read as a picture.
- Typing in a cell of a very large table costs about 15 ms a key at 3000 rows, mostly CodeMirror reparsing the table block; a key that widens a column writes every row again (about 48 ms at 3000 rows).
- Ctrl+Z after typing in two cells within half a second takes back both cells at once.
- Ctrl+Z after another writer realigned a table can take back the realignment along with the typed words.
- A count started by taking off a numbered item can run on across a blank line into a separate list in another quote.
- With two cursors in two lists, the second list can be numbered on from the first.
- A child moved up when its parent is taken off is numbered on its own instead of joining the list above.
- Whether an item taken off had a sibling above is read from the nearest line only.
- Making a list over `a` with an indented `b` under it (a lazy line) lists the indented line too.
- Unquoting a selection that holds an indented code line written as `    > prompt` takes the `>` out of the code.
- Enter on an empty nested numbered task (`- a` then `  1. [ ] `) ends the whole list instead of going back one level.
- Enter with the caret between `1. ` and the box doubles the box on the new line.
- Shift-Tab on the first child of a nested numbered list does not renumber the children left behind.
- The first Ctrl+Z in a cell after another pane left the table misaligned takes back only the realignment.
- A structural edit (rows or columns added) by another writer during an input method composition still draws the table again.
- When another writer moves rows during an input method composition, the words being composed are dropped (nothing wrong is written).
- In a read-only note, Insert column and Delete column shift dragged column widths although the table is not changed.
- A note that is only a table, with no line around it, shows the table as source after Ctrl+Z in a cell (there is no line beside the table for the note's caret), and in a note whose table has no blank line beside it the undo returns the caret to where it was.
- The first key in a cell moves the note's caret beside the table, so the marks of the line the caret was on are hidden again.
- Tab on an item indented with a tab character writes spaces before the tab and carries neither children nor numbers.
- A list toggled over a whole callout breaks the callout (`> 1. [!NOTE]`), and over a rule (`---`) lists the rule.
- The quote toggle with the caret on one line inside a code fence writes `> ` into the code.
- Quoting a lazy line of an item (`- a` then `lazy`) takes it out of the item.
- Numbering paragraphs after `1. a` and a blank line starts again at `1.`.
- Tab on a numbered item does not renumber the siblings after it.
- A change from disk that arrives during an input method composition is saved back as if typed, which writes the same words again.
- An edit from an agent within half a second of typing joins the user's undo step.
- A note dragged in from the explorer before the link code has loaded lands where the drop was in the old text, if the note changed meanwhile.
- Judged as designed, not a bug: a line under an item written without indentation (`- a` then `lazy`) is listed as its own item by the list toggles, as the existing test "keeps a nested bullet nested when the list is made over it" asks; Markdown reads it as a lazy line of the item.
- After typing above a table, the first key in a header cell draws the table again, which drops dragged column widths and the remembered sort order (the widget compares against a position that was mapped, not rebuilt).
- With the focus in a cell and nothing typed yet, rows moved by another writer leave the focus on the same row number, so the next key goes into the other record (the cell's text visibly changes first).
- A plain-text paste that waits for the clipboard is written into whatever note the pane shows by then, and does not check again for a read-only note.
- A `"` straight after `*`, `_`, `—` or `>` becomes a closing quote.
- Ticking one of two identical task lines while a line is added above can tick the other one.
- Shift-Tab on a numbered item does not renumber the outer siblings after it.
- Mod-Enter in a task inside a quote does nothing.
- A folded rendered fence (chart, diagram) can show its header twice.
- The image widget carries a code comment that was already on `unstable`; left alone as it is outside this change.
- Insert line above on a list item or quote line inserts a bare blank line, which makes the list loose or splits the quote.
- Join lines on an opening fence line pulls the first code line into the info string.
- Reformat document does not map the caret.
- On mac, Ctrl-` does nothing inside a table cell (the cell shortcuts check only Mod-Shift-`).
- A quote with `[!word]` in the middle of its first line is drawn as a callout.
- `1:100:5` shows as `1💯5` (the display side of EC12).
- A reference image `![alt][ref]` is not drawn.
- The last line of an unclosed fence is styled as its closing line.
- A fence language written in capitals (```` ```Mermaid ````) is not drawn.
- Focus mode with the caret at the start of a paragraph undims only its first line.
- Changing case on a wikilink with no alias changes its target too (note names match without regard to case).
- Plain text with no HTML pasted into a quote or item puts later paragraphs outside it (plain paste is kept as typed).
- Orden-copied text pasted with several cursors goes in whole at each cursor.
- In a table cell, a mark toggled over `1. x` or `- x` skips the would-be marker.
- Insert code block or maths over picked quoted lines puts the fence at column 0.
- Underline or comment over several blocks writes one run.
- Remove link does nothing on an autolink or bare address.
- A paragraph right under a note that starts with a `---` rule keeps straight quotes.
- A command behind a lazy door, used before its code has loaded, runs later against the state it was given.
- A quote turned into a list from the block menu gives one item per line.
- Equation numbering counts `$$` blocks inside items and quotes, which are shown as source.
- The table of contents shows inline marks in headings (`**bold**`, `[[Note|Alias]]`).
- In a link reference definition only the `:` is hidden; `[toc]` on a paragraph's second line is not drawn.
- The hover preview can open at an earlier line with the same text as the section's first line.
- A note embed with trailing spaces and the caret after them shows as plain source.
- A `[toc]` or iframe card as the last block gets no open line under it.
- A fence written as ```` ```js:app.js ```` gets no highlighting; a footnote's hover shows only its first line.
- Insert link over two paragraphs writes one link across the blank line, and does not escape brackets in its label.
- Change case alters inline maths, inline code and HTML attribute paths.
- A pasted link over a label ending in `\` escapes its own `]`.
- A diagram, chart or query fence inside an item, quote or callout is left as source but not styled as code (left undrawn by design).
- Shift-ArrowLeft from an item's words leaves the selection head before the hidden marker; on `- # Heading` with quiet marks Home stops before the hidden `# `.
- `$$a$$ ^id`, a one-line equation with a block id, is not drawn.
- `paste.ts` `readClipboard` carries a code comment that was already on `unstable`; left alone as it is outside this change.
- A note saved ending in a table and one line break: words typed on that line still become a row.
- Reverse lines in a callout moves its title line; the quote toggle over front matter quotes the `---` lines.
- Edit link on a table-cell wikilink `[[Note\|Alias]]` selects the backslash with the target (source view only).
- Toggling a mark between two runs (`a** b **c` picked) inverts the formatting; a link pasted over a selection holding a link nests them.
- Pasting several lines on a fence's info line or at the end of its closer writes them there.
- Changing case with two ranges that overlap after widening makes overlapping changes.
- Insert footnote at the start of a note holding only front matter writes the label into it.
- Typing on a blank line under an HTML block can leave a `$$` block below drawn.
- Folding with the caret at the very end of a section leaves it at the fold's end; typing right after pressing fold drops the fold.
- A quote with two spaces after `>`, with quiet marks on, can take the caret between the hidden spaces.
- ArrowDown on a wrapped last line of a note ending in a table or code adds a line.
- A sequence diagram participant with a dash (`Front-end->API`) is split wrongly.
- Fifteenth re-audit Lows: Mod-K over several paragraphs links only the words of the first; underline and comment marks nest oddly when stacked; Move block up across a numbered list leaves the numbers to the next renumber; Turn into paragraph on a callout keeps its `>` marks; pasting a row at a table's end in Source mode lands below the table's blank line; a screenshot pasted inside code is written as an image link there; Edit link on a badge image edits the outer link only; joining two headings keeps the second `#`; diagrams do not follow a theme switched while they are open; focus mode dims a list one item at a time; heading numbers start at 0.1 under a note that opens with `###`; quoted `$$` marks inside a callout are drawn as text; `[^missing]` is shown as a plain label; the embedded-image resolver is read once per draw; a query block re-runs on every save; `[a [b] c](url)` is drawn with its inner brackets; a table drawn just before typing `#` above it stays stale until the next edit.
- Sixteenth re-audit Lows: Enter on a `>`-only line in code in an item in a quote drops the item indent; Heading on an untitled callout gives `> [!NOTE]## `, and a later quote line starting `[!x]` is taken as a callout marker; bold over a whole untitled callout line wraps its `>`; Duplicate block copies its `^id`; pasting whole lines from code in an item back into it indents lines 2 and on twice; list toggles on a footnote definition turn it into an item; bold on a word inside a link inside bold nests marks; prose lines listing numbers (`Sizes: 1, 2, 3`) paste as a table; Upper case writes `&NBSP;` and recases selected front matter keys; bold across blocks where some are bold gives `****a****`; Insert block from an empty item or quote line lands at column 0 and leaves the empty marker (existing tests ask for column 0); a Markdown example block whose body holds a fence opener with a language gets a second closer on Enter after its own opener.
- Sixteenth re-audit drawing Lows: two iframes on consecutive lines draw only the first; `[docs](u "Docs")` leaves the space before the title visible; typing between the two `$` of an undrawn `$$` opener leaves blocks below stale until the next edit; `> > [!note]` styles the outer quote as a callout; task chips' today and tomorrow wording goes stale at midnight until the next edit; a slow first embed read whose section has gone could blank words a later refill showed.
- Seventeenth re-audit editing Lows: on Mac, Ctrl-Shift-backquote in a table cell writes code instead of strikethrough; Mod-U with the caret inside `<u>word</u>` writes empty tags, and over a selection nests them; taking a mark off part of a word inside an underscore run writes underscores mid-word; a slash typed as a path (` /tmp`) can be fuzzy-matched to a block; a multi-line snippet accepted in a list item or quote lands at column 0; a dropped picture is inserted mid-word.
- Seventeenth re-audit drawing Lows: a drawn embed right above a code block stays drawn until the next nearby edit after the block stops being code; a `: text` line with no term above is drawn as a definition; the web card's attribute pattern takes `data-src` as `src`; fold hinges can be missing after a background reparse until the next edit; a cell link with a title ends in a space; `$`, `:smile:` and `[^1]` show as typed in cells; `x$y$` right after a letter is drawn as maths; `[^ !]` is drawn as a footnote mark.
- Eighteenth re-audit Lows: inline code over a selection holding backticks nests them; Move block on an item inside a quote moves the whole quote, and does nothing on a footnote definition; Edit > Paste from the menu ignores a clipboard image; plain paste into indented code in an item puts later lines at column 0; a snippet trigger at the end of a URL path is offered; Title case from mid-word capitalises that letter; Turn into bullet on `2024. was` reads as a nested ordered list; a fence line inside indented code or HTML counts as an open fence for Enter; an AI ask on an unclosed `ai` fence answers inside it; `isolateHistory` is dropped when a view feeds the shared doc; cells show `%%comment%%`, a highlight's colour emoji, and `[a][r]` as typed; picture and sheet wikilinks are drawn as missing notes when the file exists; a picture path keeps its escapes; `text $$x$$ inline` shows stray `$` (as the reading view does); a bullet under a numbered item gets the top-level bullet shape; a tab after `-` stays visible before the caret; `![^x](a.png)` is no longer a picture; `link-edit.ts` still reads a reference's first URL.
- Nineteenth re-audit Lows: a fence indented 1 to 3 spaces hands Run, Copy and Ask its code with the indent on; Mod-B inside inline code writes `****` into it; Mod-B right after a closed run gives `**bold******`; pasting an Orden list at the end of an item makes the outer list loose; pasting `1. a / 2. b` on an empty `2. ` item reads `1. x / 1. a` in source; the comment toggle with a caret nests; the task date hint takes Tab inside a fence; `[x](Note\(1\).md)` is not resolved; unbolding `"quoted"` leaves the quote marks outside; `http://localhost:3000` is not a link (GFM needs a dot); a reference definition's angled address has no href; inline code padding spaces show; a click at the end of `[text][ref]` opens the source; numbered headings count headings inside quotes and items; `[text]()` in a cell shows its brackets.
- Twentieth re-audit Lows: picking another colour with the caret at the end of a coloured highlight steps out instead of recolouring; Insert callout in a quote adds a quote line; Insert page break at a line start leaves no blank line after it; Mod-I inside a word in a bold run writes `**` there; list toggles over an indented code block pull it into the item; Tab on a first child with no sibling re-indents it; a cell's bare address keeps a trailing backslash; typing before a top-level fence indented 1 to 3 spaces leaves blocks below stale; mermaid is not redrawn on a theme switch; `tel:` links have no target; fold hinges and marker folds can miss the parse edge; a footnote back jump can return to the first reference after edits; a skipped heading level still shows a 0 (`1.0.1`).
- Twenty-first re-audit Lows: a dropped PDF's link name is built after the write, against whatever note the pane then shows; Move, Duplicate and Delete block carry no user event, so they can merge into typing's undo step; a block name picked for a link can land in the note the pane then shows; reformat adds header columns for rows with extra cells, and the table model does too; a note opened with the caret at 0 on a bullet puts it before the hidden marker; clicking below a front-matter-only note with no final newline lands on its closing `---`; embeds in quotes, callouts and item lines stay links; an embed above a footnote definition, an indented list or quote, or below a comment block is not drawn; `- a\n  [toc]` draws contents inside the item; an embed with trailing spaces is never drawn; `![[Note]] ^id` is not drawn; `[[Note|**bold**]]` shows its alias stars.
- Twenty-second re-audit Lows: a `> quote` right under paragraph lines sorts in with them; Turn into quote moves a child item up to the top level; Mod-K in `[sic]`, a bare URL or an image's alt text writes `[]()` there; `## Title ##` shows a space before the hidden closing hashes; `:+1:` is not drawn as an emoji; entities and inline HTML show as typed; an embed above a comment block is not drawn; Ctrl-click on the right half of `[[A]]`'s last letter next to `[[B]]` can open B; the task date hint always shows 🛫; an unclosed `$$abc` at the very end draws as `a`; an alias row writes `[[alias]]`, which may name another note; a list pasted over a whole item nests its first marker; Delete line with carets on the last two lines leaves an empty line; Shift-Tab on a tab-indented child stays nested; Enter before a trailing `^id` moves the id to the new item; Upper case recases emoji shortcodes; Copy link to block on a reference definition breaks it; block inserts on an item's continuation line land at column 0.
- Twenty-third re-audit Lows (open): numbered tasks lose their number when drawn; pictures in table cells; the first key after opening a drawn block; the `[toc]` widget is rebuilt on each key; Home on a callout title stops before the hidden label; a removed AI change still offers Keep and Undo; Delete line keeps the column and can land inside the hidden box of the task below; a bare `#` mid-line offers every tag; backticks in pasted HTML prose are not escaped; table sort reads `10,5` as 105. Fixed with EC238 and EC239: typed marks in maths, and the Turn into caret. Still open: text with a leading newline pasted at the end of an item nests its lines.
- Twenty-fourth re-audit Lows (open): Orden-copied quote lines pasted on an empty `> ` line nest the first; items copied from a numbered list pasted on an empty bullet nest the later ones; lines copied with their indent pasted on an empty item; snippets offered inside `[[`; spreadsheet text pasted into one table cell keeps its tabs; agent `offsetOf` and `foldLine` throw on a NaN line; Delete at the end of a folded line pulls the first hidden line up; Delete line, Copy line down and Enter on folded headings or fences ignore the fold; a direct select in quiet mode can land inside hidden marks; `$5 … x$` reads as maths (pandoc rules); a delimiter row without outer pipes reads as a list; a block id on a heading hides it from `[[Note#Title]]`; YAML `#` comments can count as headings for sections.
- Twenty-fifth re-audit Lows (open): Alt+Up/Down in a numbered list does not renumber; Bold over a selection starting with a bold run nests stars; `# H` then `- b` pasted on an empty item nests; Mod-K over a selected bare address writes it as words with an empty address; whole lines ending in a newline pasted on an empty item loosen the list; a comment opening a line leaves the rest undrawn; `[x][t](u)` with no reference `t`; an item block id inside a quote embeds the whole quote; quiet-mode Backspace with several cursors; Ctrl+Left/Right stop at hidden marks; `_https://x.com_` is not a link; a backslash hard break shows its `\`.
- Twenty-sixth re-audit Lows (open): emoji completion after a `/` in prose; smart quotes inside a link reference definition title; smart punctuation inside an unclosed fence at the end of the note; Backspace at a heading right after a folded section pulls it into the hidden text; folding `> - item` in a quote hides the next sibling; ArrowLeft/Right need an extra press at hidden mark edges in quiet mode; a delimiter row edit under an embed and a front matter space leave drawing stale until the next edit.
- Twenty-seventh re-audit Lows (open): editLink on `<…>` selects the brackets; removeLink on a heading wikilink leaves `Note#H`; alias completion writes `[[Alias]]`; setCalloutSign('+') on a `-` callout; re-asking an AI fence written `><!--orden:ai-->`; an AI answer ending in a newline in a quote leaves an empty `>` line; paragraphs pasted at the start of a heading line; nested items pasted on a top-level item (ambiguous); SharedDoc `ownUndo` counts transactions; `isolateHistory` is not passed to SharedDoc; a file dropped on a drawn table (unverified); toggleQuote in maths, front matter or comments; toggleBulletList on `[ref]:` or `: def` lines; a paragraph above a table or an edited HTML start line can drift from a fresh parse; `[a [b] c](url)` drawn raw; image source escapes kept; `^a b^`; a footnote label with a space; a footnote's indented continuation drawn as code.
- Twenty-eighth re-audit Lows (open): a heading over verbatim lines plus a blank line writes an empty heading on the blank line; toggleQuote in a table row; block inserts inside an HTML block, comment or front matter; Insert TOC in a table row; Shift-Tab on a code line at the base indent in an item; an AI answer stopped inside a code block leaves its fence open; Sort lines on numbered items that change width; code pasted from an IDE as HTML; a table delimiter row edited under a paragraph or quote can drift from a fresh parse; quiet-mode carets between hidden mark characters after typing a block marker or a mark character.
- Twenty-ninth re-audit Lows (open): a lone `\r` pasted into a table cell; heading or quote commands on a footnote or definition line; a heading command on a Setext heading leaves `===`; a table pasted on an empty `> ` line under quoted words; `"` over a selection with smart punctuation and close brackets; Edit link on `[docs][missing]`; ArrowDown at the end of an unclosed fence adds a line; video and one-line frame cards reveal at either edge; a shut callout inside a list item or a nested quote; a custom callout with a title; TOC links cannot be opened with the keyboard; `:smile:` in the reading view; code lines in list items get `dir=rtl`; hover state shared across panes; `%%` comments around a heading.
- Thirtieth re-audit Lows (open): Insert footnote on an empty `> ` line at the end of the note, or mid-note when the note ends in a bare marker; a selection of only a line break pasted over keeps the break; code copied from a drawn fence in an item carries the item indent; a list pasted at the start of a paragraph line glues its last item to the words; case commands lose a backward selection; the `>` of every line but the first of a `$$` formula in a quote stays shown; a diagram keeps the old theme after a light or dark switch until redrawn; an embed above a comment block is not drawn; a multi-line web frame followed straight away by text; right-to-left lines led by a picture embed, `%%comment%%` or `[[Note|]]`; nested callouts get both colour classes; bullets inside a numbered list are drawn as top level; HTML entities and `<br>` show as typed; `bat`, `batch` and `cmd` fences get no highlighting.
- Thirty-first re-audit Lows (open): a web address pasted over a whole line that cannot become a link glues the lines; highlight over a selection holding a highlight nests the marks; a table right under a paragraph at the note's end with a broken delimiter row keeps a stale tree until the next edit; fold arrows are not rebuilt when a slow parse moves on; a single Backspace on a one-letter edge word of a multi-word run in quiet mode; deleting the space beside an `_italic_` word glues the underscore.
- Thirty-second re-audit Lows (open): Enter on an empty nested item of the other list kind leaves a lazy marker; bold over a paragraph whose second line starts `2024. `; Alt+Up/Down with a selection ending at a table's first line; `$$y$$` mid-line drawn as inline maths; Backspace at the start of a line right below a drawn maths or diagram block joins it to the closing mark; Ctrl+Backspace at the start of task words removes the box; `[](url)` with an empty label draws nothing.
- Round 33 Lows, not chased: in Source mode a paste that closes or opens a block beside a row of the same table adds a blank line; Turn into puts the caret before a newly visible marker; Duplicate on a numbered item copies its number; Move down on an item's last paragraph moves only that paragraph; pasting numeric code can be read as a table; Copy link on a fence inside a quote; Ctrl+Backspace at `1. |item` leaves `1item` (CodeMirror's default, older than round 32); with marks out of sight `- > quote` puts the caret before the hidden `> `; with marks out of sight Ctrl+Backspace at the inner start of a run at the very start of the note only moves the caret.
- Round 34 Lows, not chased: Delete or Move up on the last paragraph of a quote with words after it leaves a `>` line above them; Move on the first list item under a callout title adds a blank `>` line under the title; Copy link to block on the second line of a quoted paragraph names the whole quote; moving the last item of a callout down past the words after it makes a new quote; link suggestions with nothing typed list only the first 40 notes, and a folder name matches every note in it; in quiet mode a find match on a heading's hidden `#` moves its head to the end of the hidden run; `\n` typed in the Find box is a newline but stays literal in Replace.
- Round 35 Lows, not chased: a wikilink in a table cell is drawn as plain words and cannot be followed (designed by `table/inline.test.ts`); a line outside any quote holding `[!note]-` folds as a shut callout on the next open; Setext headings are left out of `[toc]` and heading numbers, and Copy link on one writes `===== ^id`; Copy link on `## Title ^abc` gives `#Title ^abc`; Turn into Text does nothing on a nested quote's paragraph; Move up on the second paragraph of a quote inside an item loosens the list; an AI answer streamed in a quote can write a blank line as `> ` with a trailing space; undrawn maths inside an item can keep an `orden-math-source` class after edits.
- Round 36 Lows, not chased: turning a callout into anything keeps its literal `[!note] Title`; Turn into Heading on a quote starting with a bare `>` writes no heading; Turn into Text on a quote holding a list runs the items together; Copy link on a heading inside a callout names the whole callout; Insert code block, math, callout or table on an empty item leaves the empty bullet behind; note links (`[x](Plan.md)`, `[x](#Heading)`) in table cells and note links inside embeds cannot be followed; Contents entries stop only `mousedown`, so a click still moves to `#`; drag-selecting in an unfocused cell selects nothing on the first press; subscript and superscript show as typed in cells.
- Round 37 Lows, not chased: a `\|` inside inline code in a cell makes a click after the pipe land one character early; a row with an extra trailing pipe gives the table an extra empty column on its next write; several spreadsheet cells pasted into one cell stay in that cell with tabs; hashtags, footnote links and `#heading` links inside an embed or hover card do not open; fold hinges can lag behind background parsing; a trailing `\` hard break shows its backslash in Live Preview.
- Round 38 Lows, not chased: Markdown copied from a `.md` file in VS Code pastes inside a fence; several images pasted or dropped into a list item are joined by a bare line break, so the second leaves the item; table sort does not read `-$5` as a number; in the note hover card's editable preview, a plain click on a link in a table cell or embed opens it; lezer-markdown's incremental reparse can leave a stale split paragraph at the end of a long note when an HTML block start stops being one; footnote hover and jump fail past about 100k characters beyond the viewport; an `<img>` embed keeps its old address when the image resolver changes without a doc change.
- Round 39 Lows, not chased (Move past a footnote definition right under a paragraph, a regression from EC293, is fixed with `block/footnote-def-block.test.ts`): tag completion's Enter can take a fuzzy match; pasted subscript is raw text in the editor; `<kbd>` keys paste as plain words; `agent-hands` does not clamp a column past the end of the note; a mixed copy of prose plus a monospaced `white-space: pre` block becomes one code paste.
- Round 39 drawing Lows, not chased: `[^1]: def` right under a table's last row now ends the table in Live Preview, while the reading view keeps it as a row; Enter at the end of a folded fence's opening line adds a code line on top and moves the hinge; `js{1,3}` info strings get no highlighting.
- Round 40 Lows, not chased: TeX written on the opening `$$` line over several lines is not maths in Live Preview; inline footnotes `^[...]` are not drawn. The round 40 editing audit was stopped by the pause before it reported.
- A list inside a quote is read as one block when naming or searching blocks (forty-first re-audit).
- A heading with closing hashes and an id (`## Sub ## ^id`) keeps the closing hashes in its heading text (forty-first re-audit).
- An id on its own line under a list shows as belonging to the last item in the block search list, though links to it show the whole list (forty-first re-audit).
- A link with a title, `[link](https://x.com "t")`, shows two spaces after the word, because the space before the title is in no syntax node (forty-first re-audit).
- A cancelled task `- [-]` is drawn as a bullet with a raw `[-]`, because the parser only knows `[ ]` and `[x]`; nothing in Orden writes `[-]` (forty-first re-audit).
- `[text [nested] more](url)` stays raw in the editor, though the reading view draws it as a link (forty-first re-audit).
- Inline `$$x$$` mid-line draws as `$` around inline maths, in the editor and the reading view alike (forty-first re-audit).
- A heading ending in ` ^2` loses that ending, because it is read as a block id (forty-first re-audit).
- A fold whose animation is still running when a key is typed stays open (forty-first re-audit).
- The block completion lists front matter as three blocks (`---`, its lines, `---`); picking one does nothing (forty-second re-audit).
- A lazy continuation line (`- milk\nlazy`) is named or embedded alone, not with its item (forty-second re-audit).
- A fence inside a list item shows as its own "```" row in the block completion; picking it names the item (forty-second re-audit).
- An own-line id after blank lines below indented code is listed as an empty, unnamed block; linking still works (forty-second re-audit).
- A line typed right under a table is a table row, but naming the table writes the id after that line (forty-third re-audit).
- A lazy line in a callout (`> body\nlazy`) is listed and named on its own (forty-third re-audit).
- Naming `<details>` puts an own-line id inside the details block (forty-third re-audit).
- `words\n$$...$$` and `words\n<div>...</div>` are named as one block with the words (forty-third re-audit).
- An empty `<li>` makes a pasted list loose, and Wikipedia citations paste as `^[\[1\]](#cite_note-1)^` (forty-third re-audit).
- Tab or Shift-Tab on a numbered item leaves the siblings' numbers unrenumbered in the source; they still draw right (forty-third re-audit).
- Typing in the hover card of a CRLF note writes the note back with LF endings (forty-third re-audit).
- An own-line id after a bullet list followed by a numbered list embeds both lists (forty-third re-audit).
- An unclosed `$$` at the end of a note draws without its last line, and `![[Note]] ^x` is not drawn as an embed block (forty-third re-audit).
- Checked and safe: a peer caret colour is written into a style attribute, but every colour comes from Orden's fixed accent palette.
- Folds restored from a session by line number are not checked against a changed file.
- A typed `\\|` in a table cell splits the cell on read-back.
- `drag-start.test.ts` "selects from the press, when no drag starts" failed once in 55 runs, while other test runs loaded the machine, and passed the 54 others and the full suite. The timer it waits for runs first by Node's timer order, so the likely cause is a jsdom selection event arriving late under load. It is test-only and was not reproduced again.

Out of area, reported to the coordinator: the AI sidebar (`ai-sidebar/src/drawn.ts`) passes an HTML-escaped picture address to `notePicture` without `attributeText`, so a picture named with `&` does not load there.
